- [x] **Step 4: Implémenter**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-version.ts` :
```ts
import { type GrantedPermissions, permissionList } from "@kibo/schema";

const parts = (v: string) => v.split(".").map((n) => Number.parseInt(n, 10));
const signature = (p: GrantedPermissions) => permissionList(p).sort().join("|");

export function bumpVersion(v: string, part: "minor" | "patch"): string {
  const [major = 0, minor = 0, patch = 0] = parts(v);
  return part === "minor" ? `${major}.${minor + 1}.0` : `${major}.${minor}.${patch + 1}`;
}

export function proposeVersion(
  base: { version: string; permissions: GrantedPermissions; configVersion: number } | null,
  next: { permissions: GrantedPermissions; configVersion: number },
): string {
  if (!base) return "0.1.0";
  const same = signature(base.permissions) === signature(next.permissions) && base.configVersion === next.configVersion;
  return bumpVersion(base.version, same ? "patch" : "minor");
}
```

`packages/daemon/src/ai/draft-publish.ts` :
```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import {
  addedPermissions,
  type ComponentDraft,
  type ComponentDraftDetails,
  compareSemver,
  type FinalizeComponentDraftInput,
  type FinalizeResult,
  formatRef,
  grantedOf,
  KiboError,
  type PublishPreview,
  type ReviewComponentDraftInput,
} from "@kibo/schema";
import {
  agentFiles,
  type DraftPaths,
  draftPaths,
  installDraft,
  readDraftManifest,
  removeDraft,
  writeDraftManifest,
} from "./draft-files";
import { applyDraftEvent, type DraftEvent } from "./draft-machine";
import type { DraftStore } from "./draft-store";
import { proposeVersion } from "./draft-version";
import type { AiEvents, Clock, ComponentCatalog, Devkit, Differ, ProjectAccess } from "./ports";

export type PublishDeps = {
  store: DraftStore;
  devkit: Devkit;
  catalog: ComponentCatalog;
  projects: ProjectAccess;
  differ: Differ;
  events: AiEvents;
  clock: Clock;
  home: string;
};

const firstLine = (text: string) => (text.split("\n")[0] ?? "").trim().slice(0, 200);
const same = (a: string, b: string) => Buffer.compare(readFileSync(a), readFileSync(b)) === 0;
const visible = (d: ComponentDraft) => d.status === "review" || d.status === "permissions";

export function createDraftPublisher(deps: PublishDeps) {
  const busy = new Set<string>();
  const paths = (d: ComponentDraft): DraftPaths => draftPaths(deps.home, d.id);
  const apply = (d: ComponentDraft, e: DraftEvent) => {
    const next = applyDraftEvent(d, e, deps.clock.now());
    deps.store.save(next);
    deps.events.publish({ type: "draft.changed", draftId: next.id, status: next.status });
    return next;
  };
  const exclusive = async <T>(id: string, work: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> => {
    if (busy.has(id)) throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", "this draft is already being processed");
    busy.add(id);
    try {
      return await work();
    } finally {
      busy.delete(id);
    }
  };

  const diffOf = async (d: ComponentDraft) => {
    const p = paths(d);
    const out = [];
    for (const path of agentFiles(p, d.withServer)) {
      const before = existsSync(join(p.baseDir, path)) ? join(p.baseDir, path) : null;
      const after = existsSync(join(p.dir, path)) ? join(p.dir, path) : null;
      if (before && after && same(before, after)) continue;
      out.push(await deps.differ({ path, before, after }));
    }
    return out;
  };

  const previewOf = async (d: ComponentDraft): Promise<PublishPreview> => {
    const p = paths(d);
    const manifest = readDraftManifest(p.dir);
    const validation = deps.store.report(d.id);
    if (!validation) throw new KiboError("INTERNAL", `draft ${d.id} has no validation report`);
    const latest = d.mode === "modify" ? deps.catalog.latest(d.componentId) : null;
    const granted = grantedOf(manifest);
    const reviewed = d.status === "permissions";
    const proposed = proposeVersion(
      latest
        ? { version: latest.version, permissions: grantedOf(latest.manifest), configVersion: latest.manifest.configVersion }
        : null,
      { permissions: granted, configVersion: manifest.configVersion },
    );
    return {
      id: d.componentId,
      title: manifest.title,
      from: latest?.version ?? null,
      to: reviewed ? manifest.version : proposed,
      hash: reviewed ? await deps.devkit.hash(p.dir) : null,
      status: latest ? "update" : "new",
      usages: latest ? deps.catalog.usages(d.componentId) : [],
      changes: reviewed ? manifest.changes : d.mode === "modify" ? [firstLine(d.description)] : [],
      newPermissions: addedPermissions(latest?.granted ?? null, granted),
      migration: null,
      validation,
    };
  };

  const details = async (draftId: string): Promise<ComponentDraftDetails> => {
    const d = deps.store.get(draftId);
    const shown = visible(d);
    return {
      ...d,
      report: deps.store.report(draftId),
      diff: shown ? await diffOf(d) : [],
      manifest: shown ? readDraftManifest(paths(d).dir) : null,
      publish: shown ? await previewOf(d) : null,
    };
  };

  const checkSource = async (d: ComponentDraft, p: DraftPaths, src: string, hash: string) => {
    if (d.mode === "create" && existsSync(src) && (await deps.devkit.hash(src)) !== hash)
      throw new KiboError("CONFLICT", `components/src/${d.componentId} exists`);
    if (d.mode === "modify") {
      const current = await deps.devkit.hash(src);
      if (current !== (await deps.devkit.hash(p.baseDir)) && current !== hash)
        throw new KiboError("CONFLICT", "the component source changed since the draft started");
    }
  };

  return {
    details,

    review: (input: ReviewComponentDraftInput) =>
      exclusive(input.draftId, async () => {
        const d = deps.store.get(input.draftId);
        if (d.status !== "review") throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", `draft is ${d.status}`);
        const latest = d.mode === "modify" ? deps.catalog.latest(d.componentId) : null;
        if (latest && compareSemver(input.version, latest.version) <= 0)
          throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", `version must be greater than ${latest.version}`);
        const dir = paths(d).dir;
        writeDraftManifest(dir, { ...readDraftManifest(dir), version: input.version, changes: input.changes });
        apply(d, { type: "reviewed" });
        return details(input.draftId);
      }),

    finalize: (input: FinalizeComponentDraftInput): Promise<FinalizeResult> =>
      exclusive(input.draftId, async () => {
        const d = deps.store.get(input.draftId);
        if (d.status !== "permissions") throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", `draft is ${d.status}`);
        const p = paths(d);
        if (readDraftManifest(p.dir).version !== input.version)
          throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", "version differs from the reviewed one");
        if ((await deps.devkit.hash(p.dir)) !== input.hash) throw new KiboError("HASH_MISMATCH", "the draft changed since review");
        if (input.target && !deps.projects.pageExists(input.target.projectId, input.target.pageId))
          throw new KiboError("NOT_FOUND", "target page not found");
        const src = deps.catalog.sourceDir(d.componentId);
        await checkSource(d, p, src, input.hash);
        const install = installDraft(p.dir, src);
        let publish: FinalizeResult["publish"];
        try {
          publish = await deps.catalog.publish({ id: d.componentId, strategy: input.strategy, origin: "ai" });
        } catch (e) {
          install.rollback();
          throw e;
        }
        install.commit();
        if (publish.version.hash !== input.hash)
          throw new KiboError("HASH_MISMATCH", "the published sources differ from the reviewed draft");
        const version = publish.needsApproval
          ? await deps.catalog.approve({ id: d.componentId, version: publish.version.version, hash: publish.version.hash, trust: input.trust })
          : publish.version;
        const instance = input.target
          ? await deps.projects.addInstance(input.target.projectId, input.target.pageId, formatRef(d.componentId, version.version))
          : null;
        apply(d, { type: "finalized" });
        removeDraft(p);
        return { publish, version, instanceId: instance?.id ?? null };
      }),
  };
}
```
Reprise après un échec partiel : si `approve` ou `addInstance` échoue, le brouillon reste `permissions` ; une nouvelle finalisation retrouve une source identique au brouillon (acceptée par `checkSource`) et `Publisher.publish` rend la version existante pour une même empreinte (`status: "unchanged"`, C3), approuvée seulement si elle ne l'est pas encore. Une instance déjà ajoutée avant l'échec reste ; la seconde tentative en ajoute une autre : accepté (l'utilisateur la supprime), noté dans les risques du jalon.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/ui/src/ai/ModifyWithAiDialog.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/ai/modify.test.tsx`
- Modify: `packages/ui/src/components-page/ComponentRowMenu.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components-page/ComponentsPage.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components-page/components-page.test.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/components-page/rows.ts`, `packages/ui/src/pages/InstanceMenu.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/pages/instance.test.tsx`, `packages/ui/src/shell/lazy-dialogs.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AiDraftPanel`, `useAiAvailability` (T12) ; RPC `startComponentDraft` (mode `modify`) ; `fr.ai.modify*` (T1), `fr.components.origin` (phase 4) ; `ComponentRow.origin` (`components-page/rows.ts`), `ComponentSummary.versions[].origin` via `useComponents()` (C5) ; `lazyPanel` (`@kibo/sdk`).
- Produces: `type ModifyTarget = { id: string; title: string; version: string; origin: "user" | "ai" }` ; `ModifyWithAiDialog({ component, open, onOpenChange }: { component: ModifyTarget; open: boolean; onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void })` ; `modifiable(origin: string): origin is "user" | "ai"` (`components-page/rows.ts`, réexporté par le dialogue) ; `ComponentRowMenu` prend `onModifyWithAi(target: ModifyTarget): void` ; `lazy-dialogs.ts` exporte `ModifyWithAiDialog` (différé).

Contexte réel : l'état du dialogue vit dans `ComponentsPage` et dans `InstanceMenu` (qui gèrent déjà `PublishDialog`/`TrustDialog` de la même façon) ; `Shell.tsx` n'est pas touché. `ModifyWithAiDialog` tire `AiDraftPanel`, `DiffView` et `TrustDialog` : il est toujours chargé via `lazy-dialogs.ts`, jamais importé directement (I29) ; la règle `ai\/[A-Za-z]+` ajoutée à `bundle-report.ts` par T12 le couvre.

`packages/ui/src/ai/ModifyWithAiDialog.tsx` :
```tsx
import { Button } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/button";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/dialog";
import { Label } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/label";
import { Textarea } from "@kibo/sdk/ui/textarea";
import { Bot } from "lucide-react";
import { type FormEvent, useId, useState } from "react";
import { client } from "../api";
import { fr } from "../i18n/fr";
import { AiDraftPanel } from "./AiDraftPanel";
import { useAiAvailability } from "./use-ai-availability";

export type ModifyTarget = { id: string; title: string; version: string; origin: "user" | "ai" };
export { modifiable } from "../components-page/rows";

type Props = {
  component: ModifyTarget;
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void;
};

export function ModifyWithAiDialog({ component, open, onOpenChange }: Props) {
  const id = useId();
  const { ready, block } = useAiAvailability("generateur");
  const [request, setRequest] = useState("");
  const [draftId, setDraftId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const valid = request.trim().length >= 5 && request.trim().length <= 2000;

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const draft = await client.rpc({
        method: "startComponentDraft",
        draft: { mode: "modify", id: component.id, description: request.trim() },
      });
      setDraftId(draft.id);
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : fr.common.error);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent className={draftId ? "sm:max-w-3xl" : "sm:max-w-lg"}>
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>{fr.ai.modifyTitle(component.title)}</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>{fr.ai.modifySubtitle(component.version, fr.components.origin[component.origin])}</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        {draftId ? (
          <AiDraftPanel draftId={draftId} target={null} onDone={() => onOpenChange(false)} />
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={submit} className="grid gap-3">
            <Label htmlFor={`${id}-request`}>{fr.ai.modifyField}</Label>
            <Textarea id={`${id}-request`} rows={4} maxLength={2000} value={request} onChange={(e) => setRequest(e.target.value)} />
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{fr.ai.modifyHelp}</p>
            {block && <p className="text-xs text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">{fr.ai.blocked[block]}</p>}
            {error && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-destructive">{error}</p>}
            <DialogFooter>
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}>
                {fr.common.cancel}
              </Button>
              <Button type="submit" variant="agent" disabled={!ready || block !== null || !valid || busy}>
                <Bot className="size-4" /> {fr.ai.launch}
              </Button>
            </DialogFooter>
          </form>
        )}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}
```
La modification est publiée sans cible (`target: null`) : les instances suivent la stratégie choisie à l'écran 6 (« Mettre à jour partout » ou « Créer une nouvelle version »).

### Task 17: Branchement sur les phases 2 à 5 et RPC

**Tâche à risque** : relue par `kibo-lead` avant intégration (lancement de processus, script `kibo` dans le `PATH` du run, garde-fou branché sur le récepteur de hooks, profils système au démarrage, aiguillage des RPC).

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/daemon/src/ai/methods.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/methods.test.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/adapters.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/adapters.test.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/bootstrap.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/testing/harness.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/ai/onboarding.int.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/daemon/src/service.ts` (`attachAi`, aiguillage), `packages/daemon/src/server.ts` (`AI_UNAVAILABLE: 503`), `packages/daemon/src/daemon.ts` (`agentEnv`, `assistantTimeoutMs`, démarrage de l'IA), `packages/daemon/src/components/service.ts` (expose `registry` et `publisher`), `packages/daemon/src/code/read.ts` (exporte `DIFF_FLAGS`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: T1 à T15, T20 (`ensureSystemProfiles(docs)`, profils `assistant`/`generateur` avec `enabled`), T21 (`Publisher.publish(id, strategy, { origin })`) ; A1 à A9, B2, B3, C1, C3, C4, D1.
- Produces (`methods.ts`) : `AI_METHODS: ReadonlySet<string>` ; `isAiRequest(req: RpcRequest): req is AiRpcRequest` ; `type AiPort = { handle(req: AiRpcRequest): Promise<unknown> }` ; `createAiRpc(deps: AiRpcDeps): AiPort` avec `AiRpcDeps = { ai: AiAvailability; environment: () => Promise<Environment>; starter: { suggest(input: { role: Role; text: string }): { runId: string } }; lifecycle: DraftLifecycle; publisher: DraftPublisher }` et `DraftPublisher = ReturnType<typeof createDraftPublisher>`.
- Produces (`adapters.ts`) : `systemClock: Clock` ; `createExecPort(env: Record<string, string | undefined>): Exec` ; `runsFromOrchestrator(o: Pick<Orchestrator, "submit" | "cancel" | "state" | "onRunState">): AgentRuns` ; `kiboShimArgv(opts?: { compiled?: boolean; execPath?: string }): string[]` ; `writeKiboShim(binDir: string, argv: string[]): string` ; `catalogEntries(list: ComponentSummary[]): CatalogEntry[]` ; `latestPublished(list: ComponentSummary[], id: string): PublishedComponent | null` ; `usagesOf(list: ComponentSummary[], id: string): PublishUsage[]` ; `githubConnected(statuses: IntegrationStatus[]): boolean`.
- Produces (`bootstrap.ts`) : `type AiBootstrapDeps = { home: string; toolchain: Toolchain; db: Database; docs: Docs; orchestrator: Orchestrator; registry: RegistryService; publisher: Publisher; validate: ((dir: string, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<ValidationReport>) | null; claudeBin: string | null; agentEnv: Record<string, string | undefined>; address: string; listIntegrations: () => Promise<IntegrationStatus[]>; assistantTimeoutMs?: number }` ; `startAi(deps: AiBootstrapDeps): Promise<{ port: AiPort; stop(): Promise<void> }>`.
- Produces (démon) : `Service.attachAi(port: AiPort): () => void` ; `DaemonOptions.agentEnv?: Record<string, string | undefined>` (I37 : environnement des runs d'agent et du sondage de `claude`, par défaut `process.env`) et `DaemonOptions.assistantTimeoutMs?: number` ; `ComponentsService.registry`, `ComponentsService.publisher`.
- Produces (`testing/harness.ts`) : `startAiHarness(opts: { scenario: string; claudeBin?: string; assistantTimeoutMs?: number }): Promise<AiHarness>` avec `AiHarness = { home: string; fakeState: string; rpc<R extends RpcRequest>(req: R): Promise<RpcResult[R["method"]]>; events: ChangeMessage[]; waitFor(pred: (e: ChangeMessage) => boolean, ms?: number): Promise<ChangeMessage>; waitDraft(draftId: string, status: DraftStatus, ms?: number): Promise<ComponentDraftDetails>; stop(): Promise<void> }`.

Contexte réel :
- Pas de `RunQueue` ni de `createDaemon` : les runs passent par `Orchestrator.submit` (I16), le démon se compose dans `assemble` (`daemon.ts`) et l'orchestrateur est créé **après** le serveur et les composants ; l'IA démarre juste après `service.attachAgents(orchestrator)`.
- `TaskInput.guard` reçoit `{ tool, input }` et rend `GuardDecision | null` avec `reason: string` obligatoire ; le port de T1 parle `{ toolName, toolInput }` : l'adaptateur traduit. `RunView.output` (ligne `result` brute) devient `RunEnd.stdout`. `state().runs` sert de `get`.
- Le binaire `claude` se résout par `resolveClaudeBin(opts.claudeBin, agentEnv)` (I20) ; `AGENT_CLI_NOT_FOUND` ⇒ sondage « introuvable » sans exception. Le sondage (`--version`, `--help`, `auth status --json`) s'exécute avec `agentEnv`, comme les runs.
- Commande `kibo` de l'agent (I27) : le démon ne lit la CLI que par chemin. Binaire compilé : le point d'entrée unique aiguille `component …` (`apps/desktop/sidecar/entry.ts`) ⇒ `[process.execPath]` ; en dev : `[process.execPath, <repo>/packages/cli/src/bin.ts]` (chemin calculé depuis `import.meta.dir`, sans importer `@kibo/cli`). Le run reçoit `PATH = <KIBO_HOME>/bin:<PATH de agentEnv>` et `KIBO_TOOLCHAIN = toolchain.root` (la CLI résout sa toolchain par cette variable).
- `copySource` (T4) ignore le lien `node_modules` créé par `scaffold` ; les lectures de l'agent dans le SDK passent par `readRoots = [realpath(<toolchain>/node_modules/@kibo/sdk)]` (I5) ; `validateComponent` travaille sur une copie liée à la toolchain (I26).
- `DIFF_FLAGS` vit dans `code/read.ts` sans être exporté : l'exporter (un mot), sans autre changement (I34).

import { expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { AI_RPC, KiboError, type RpcRequest } from "@kibo/schema";
import { AI_METHODS, createAiRpc, isAiRequest } from "./methods";

const draftId = "0b5c1f3e-7a51-4d2a-9c1e-2f0d6f1b8a11";

function setup() {
  const seen: string[] = [];
  const mark = (name: string) => {
    seen.push(name);
    return null as never;
  };
  const port = createAiRpc({
    ai: { status: () => mark("status"), capabilities: () => null, refresh: async () => mark("refresh") },
    environment: async () => mark("environment"),
    starter: { suggest: () => mark("suggest") },
    lifecycle: {
      start: async () => mark("start"),
      retry: () => {
        throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", "no");
      },
      revalidate: async () => mark("revalidate"),
      abandon: () => mark("abandon"),
      openFolder: async () => mark("openFolder"),
      list: () => mark("list"),
      recover: async () => {},
      idle: async () => {},
    },
    publisher: {
      details: async () => mark("details"),
      review: async () => mark("review"),
      finalize: async () => mark("finalize"),
    },
  });
  return { port, seen };
}

test("every AI method is recognised, the others are not", () => {
  expect(AI_METHODS.size).toBe(AI_RPC.length);
  expect(isAiRequest({ method: "listProjects" })).toBe(false);
  expect(isAiRequest({ method: "listComponentDrafts" })).toBe(true);
});

test("routes every AI method to its module", async () => {
  const { port, seen } = setup();
  const requests: RpcRequest[] = [
    { method: "getAiStatus" },
    { method: "getEnvironment" },
    { method: "suggestStarter", role: "other", text: "x" },
    { method: "startComponentDraft", draft: { mode: "modify", id: "burndown", description: "Ajoute un titre" } },
    { method: "revalidateComponentDraft", draftId },
    { method: "getComponentDraft", draftId },
    { method: "listComponentDrafts" },
    { method: "reviewComponentDraft", draftId, version: "0.1.1", changes: [] },
    { method: "finalizeComponentDraft", draftId, version: "0.1.1", hash: "a".repeat(64), trust: "sandboxed", strategy: "update-all", target: null },
    { method: "abandonComponentDraft", draftId },
    { method: "openComponentDraftFolder", draftId },
  ];
  for (const r of requests) if (isAiRequest(r)) await port.handle(r);
  expect(seen).toEqual(["status", "environment", "suggest", "start", "revalidate", "details", "list", "review", "finalize", "abandon", "openFolder"]);
});

test("a synchronous KiboError becomes a rejection", async () => {
  const { port } = setup();
  await expect(port.handle({ method: "retryComponentDraft", draftId })).rejects.toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
});

import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { mkdtempSync, rmSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";

});

  test("runs a binary with the given environment", async () => {

    expect(kiboShimArgv({ compiled: true, execPath: "/Applications/Kibo.app/kibo" })).toEqual(["/Applications/Kibo.app/kibo"]);
    const dev = kiboShimArgv({ compiled: false, execPath: "/usr/local/bin/bun" });

    expect(r?.stdout).toBe("kibo's component test .\n");
  });
});

function fakeOrchestrator() {
  const runs = new Map<string, RunView>();
  const listeners = new Set<(r: RunView) => void>();
  const submitted: TaskInput[] = [];
  const cancelled: string[] = [];
  const view = (id: string, state: RunView["state"], output: string | null = null): RunView => ({
    ...(runs.get(id) ?? ({} as RunView)),
    id,
    state,
    sessionId: "s1",
    error: state === "failed" ? "exit 1" : null,
    output,
  });
  const emit = (run: RunView) => {
    runs.set(run.id, run);
    for (const l of [...listeners]) l(run);
  };
  const o = {
    submit: (task: TaskInput) => {
      submitted.push(task);
      const run = view(`r${submitted.length}`, "queued");
      runs.set(run.id, run);
      return run;
    },
    cancel: (id: string) => {
      cancelled.push(id);
      const run = view(id, "cancelled");
      emit(run);
      return run;
    },
    state: (): AgentsState => ({ runs: [...runs.values()], queue: [], host: {} as AgentsState["host"], tokensToday: 0 }),
    onRunState: (l: (r: RunView) => void) => {
      listeners.add(l);
      return () => listeners.delete(l);
    },
  };
  return { o, submitted, cancelled, view, emit };
}

describe("runsFromOrchestrator", () => {
  const request = {
    profileId: "generateur" as const,
    label: "Composant Burndown",
    cwd: "/tmp/draft",
    prompt: "p",
    args: ["--tools", "Read"],
    env: { PATH: "/kibo/bin" },
    resumeSessionId: null,
    guard: ({ toolName }: { toolName: string }) =>
      toolName === "Write" ? ({ decision: "deny", reason: "reserved" } as const) : ({ decision: "allow" } as const),
  };

  test("submits a ticketless task and translates the guard", () => {
    const f = fakeOrchestrator();
    const ports = runsFromOrchestrator(f.o);
    expect(ports.enqueue(request)).toBe("r1");
    const task = f.submitted[0];
    expect(task).toMatchObject({ profileId: "generateur", projectId: null, title: "Composant Burndown", extraArgs: ["--tools", "Read"], env: { PATH: "/kibo/bin" } });
    expect(task && "resumeSessionId" in task).toBe(false);
    expect(task?.guard?.({ tool: "Write", input: {} })).toEqual({ decision: "deny", reason: "reserved" });
    expect(task?.guard?.({ tool: "Read", input: {} })).toEqual({ decision: "allow", reason: "" });
    ports.enqueue({ ...request, resumeSessionId: "s1" });
    expect(f.submitted[1]?.resumeSessionId).toBe("s1");
  });

  test("end events fire once, late subscribers get the result, output becomes stdout", () => {
    const f = fakeOrchestrator();
    const ports = runsFromOrchestrator(f.o);
    const runId = ports.enqueue(request);
    const ends: string[] = [];
    ports.onEnd(runId, (e) => ends.push(`${e.state}:${e.stdout}`));
    f.emit(f.view(runId, "running"));
    f.emit(f.view(runId, "done", '{"type":"result"}'));
    f.emit(f.view(runId, "done", '{"type":"result"}'));
    ports.onEnd(runId, (e) => ends.push(`late:${e.state}`));
    expect(ends).toEqual(['done:{"type":"result"}', "late:done"]);
    expect(ports.state(runId)).toBe("done");
  });

  test("cancel of a finished or unknown run does nothing", () => {
    const f = fakeOrchestrator();
    const ports = runsFromOrchestrator(f.o);
    const runId = ports.enqueue(request);
    f.emit(f.view(runId, "done"));
    ports.cancel(runId);
    ports.cancel("unknown");
    expect(f.cancelled).toEqual([]);
  });
});

const version = (v: Partial<ComponentVersionSummary> & { version: string }): ComponentVersionSummary => ({
  hash: "a".repeat(64),
  trust: "sandboxed",
  origin: "ai",
  active: true,
  tampered: false,
  manifest: ComponentManifest.parse({ id: "burndown", version: v.version, kind: "widget", title: "Burndown", reads: ["ticket"], writes: [] }),
  usages: [],
  ...v,
});

describe("catalog helpers", () => {
  const list: ComponentSummary[] = [
    {
      id: "kanban",
      title: "Kanban",
      builtin: true,
      versions: [version({ version: "1.0.0", origin: "kibo", manifest: ComponentManifest.parse({ id: "kanban", version: "1.0.0", kind: "both", title: "Kanban", description: "Colonnes", reads: ["ticket"], writes: [] }) })],
    },
    {

    },
    {

      versions: [

  test("catalogEntries keeps active components, never mcp-source nor adapters", () => {

  });

    ]);
  });

  test("githubConnected", () => {
    const base = { account: null, servers: [], error: null, resumeAt: null };
    expect(githubConnected([{ ...base, id: "github", state: "connected" }])).toBe(true);
    expect(githubConnected([{ ...base, id: "github", state: "active" }])).toBe(true);
    expect(githubConnected([{ ...base, id: "github", state: "disconnected" }])).toBe(false);
    expect(githubConnected([])).toBe(false);
  });
});

- [x] **Step 4: Implémenter l'aiguillage et les adaptateurs**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/methods.ts` :
```ts
import { AI_RPC, type AiRpcRequest, type Environment, type Role, type RpcRequest } from "@kibo/schema";
import type { DraftLifecycle } from "./draft-lifecycle";
import type { createDraftPublisher } from "./draft-publish";
import type { AiAvailability } from "./ports";

export type DraftPublisher = ReturnType<typeof createDraftPublisher>;
export type AiPort = { handle(req: AiRpcRequest): Promise<unknown> };
export type AiRpcDeps = {
  ai: AiAvailability;
  environment: () => Promise<Environment>;
  starter: { suggest(input: { role: Role; text: string }): { runId: string } };
  lifecycle: DraftLifecycle;
  publisher: DraftPublisher;
};

export const AI_METHODS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(AI_RPC.map((s) => s.shape.method.value));

export const isAiRequest = (req: RpcRequest): req is AiRpcRequest => AI_METHODS.has(req.method);

export function createAiRpc(deps: AiRpcDeps): AiPort {
  const route = (req: AiRpcRequest): unknown => {
    switch (req.method) {
      case "getAiStatus":
        return deps.ai.status();
      case "getEnvironment":
        return deps.environment();
      case "suggestStarter":
        return deps.starter.suggest({ role: req.role, text: req.text });
      case "startComponentDraft":
        return deps.lifecycle.start(req.draft);
      case "retryComponentDraft":
        return deps.lifecycle.retry(req.draftId);
      case "revalidateComponentDraft":
        return deps.lifecycle.revalidate(req.draftId);
      case "getComponentDraft":
        return deps.publisher.details(req.draftId);
      case "listComponentDrafts":
        return deps.lifecycle.list();
      case "reviewComponentDraft":
        return deps.publisher.review({ draftId: req.draftId, version: req.version, changes: req.changes });
      case "finalizeComponentDraft": {
        const { method: _, ...input } = req;
        return deps.publisher.finalize(input);
      }
      case "abandonComponentDraft":
        return deps.lifecycle.abandon(req.draftId);
      case "openComponentDraftFolder":
        return deps.lifecycle.openFolder(req.draftId);
    }
  };
  return { handle: async (req) => route(req) };
}
```
Si Biome refuse la variable `_` inutilisée, passer `req` tel quel (`FinalizeComponentDraftInput` n'est pas strict : le champ `method` en trop est ignoré par le type structurel).

`packages/daemon/src/ai/adapters.ts` :
```ts
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { isCompiled } from "@kibo/devkit";
import {
  type ComponentSummary,
  type ComponentVersionSummary,
  compareSemver,
  grantedOf,
  type IntegrationStatus,
  NO_PERMISSIONS,
  type PublishUsage,
  type RunView,
} from "@kibo/schema";
import type { Orchestrator } from "../agents/orchestrator-types";
import type { AgentRuns, CatalogEntry, Clock, Exec, Guard, PublishedComponent, RunEnd, RunState } from "./ports";

const CLI_ENTRY = join(import.meta.dir, "..", "..", "..", "cli", "src", "bin.ts");
const NOT_A_STARTER = new Set(["mcp-source"]);
const TERMINAL = new Set<RunState>(["done", "failed", "cancelled"]);

export const systemClock: Clock = {
  now: () => Date.now(),
  setTimeout: (fn, ms) => {
    const t = setTimeout(fn, ms);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  },
};

export function createExecPort(env: Record<string, string | undefined>): Exec {
  return async (argv, timeoutMs) => {
    const [bin, ...args] = argv;
    if (!bin) return null;
    const resolved = bin.includes("/") ? (existsSync(bin) ? bin : null) : Bun.which(bin, { PATH: env.PATH ?? "" });
    if (!resolved) return null;
    const proc = Bun.spawn([resolved, ...args], {
      env: { ...env, NO_COLOR: "1" },
      stdin: "ignore",
      stdout: "pipe",
      stderr: "pipe",
    });
    const timer = setTimeout(() => proc.kill(), timeoutMs);
    try {
      const [stdout, stderr, code] = await Promise.all([
        new Response(proc.stdout).text(),
        new Response(proc.stderr).text(),
        proc.exited,
      ]);
      return { code, stdout, stderr };
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer);
    }
  };
}

const toolGuard = (guard: Guard) => (input: { tool: string; input: Record<string, unknown> }) => {
  const d = guard({ toolName: input.tool, toolInput: input.input });
  return d.decision === "allow" ? { decision: "allow" as const, reason: "" } : d;
};

const toEnd = (run: RunView): RunEnd => ({
  state: run.state === "done" ? "done" : run.state === "cancelled" ? "cancelled" : "failed",
  sessionId: run.sessionId,
  stdout: run.output ?? "",
  error: run.error,
});

export function runsFromOrchestrator(o: Pick<Orchestrator, "submit" | "cancel" | "state" | "onRunState">): AgentRuns {
  const find = (runId: string) => o.state().runs.find((r) => r.id === runId) ?? null;
  return {
    enqueue: (req) =>
      o.submit({
        profileId: req.profileId,
        projectId: null,
        title: req.label,
        cwd: req.cwd,
        prompt: req.prompt,
        extraArgs: req.args,
        env: req.env,
        ...(req.resumeSessionId !== null && { resumeSessionId: req.resumeSessionId }),
        guard: toolGuard(req.guard),
      }).id,
    cancel(runId) {
      const run = find(runId);
      if (run && !TERMINAL.has(run.state)) o.cancel(runId);
    },
    state: (runId) => find(runId)?.state ?? null,
    onState: (runId, listener) =>
      o.onRunState((run) => {
        if (run.id === runId) listener(run.state);
      }),
    onEnd(runId, listener) {
      const now = find(runId);
      if (now && TERMINAL.has(now.state)) {
        listener(toEnd(now));
        return () => {};
      }
      let fired = false;
      const off = o.onRunState((run) => {
        if (fired || run.id !== runId || !TERMINAL.has(run.state)) return;
        fired = true;
        off();
        listener(toEnd(run));
      });
      return off;
    },
  };
}

export function kiboShimArgv(opts: { compiled?: boolean; execPath?: string } = {}): string[] {
  const execPath = opts.execPath ?? process.execPath;
  return (opts.compiled ?? isCompiled()) ? [execPath] : [execPath, CLI_ENTRY];
}

export function writeKiboShim(binDir: string, argv: string[]): string {
  mkdirSync(binDir, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const quoted = argv.map((a) => `'${a.replaceAll("'", "'\\''")}'`).join(" ");
  writeFileSync(join(binDir, "kibo"), `#!/bin/sh\nexec ${quoted} "$@"\n`, { mode: 0o700 });
  return binDir;
}

const highest = (versions: ComponentVersionSummary[]) =>
  [...versions].sort((a, b) => compareSemver(b.version, a.version))[0] ?? null;

export function catalogEntries(list: ComponentSummary[]): CatalogEntry[] {
  return list.flatMap((c) => {
    if (NOT_A_STARTER.has(c.id)) return [];
    const m = highest(c.versions.filter((v) => v.active && v.manifest !== null))?.manifest ?? null;
    if (!m || m.kind === "adapter") return [];
    return [{ id: c.id, title: m.title, description: m.description ?? "", kind: m.kind }];
  });
}

export function latestPublished(list: ComponentSummary[], id: string): PublishedComponent | null {
  const summary = list.find((c) => c.id === id && !c.builtin);
  const v = summary ? highest(summary.versions.filter((x) => x.manifest !== null)) : null;
  if (!v?.manifest) return null;
  return { version: v.version, manifest: v.manifest, granted: v.active ? grantedOf(v.manifest) : NO_PERMISSIONS, origin: v.origin };
}

export function usagesOf(list: ComponentSummary[], id: string): PublishUsage[] {
  return list
    .filter((c) => c.id === id)
    .flatMap((c) => c.versions.flatMap((v) => v.usages.map((u) => ({ ...u, version: v.version }))));
}

export function githubConnected(statuses: IntegrationStatus[]): boolean {
  return statuses.some((s) => s.id === "github" && (s.state === "connected" || s.state === "active"));
}
```
Le dernier `highest` de `latestPublished` inclut les versions non approuvées : une modification part toujours de la version la plus haute, et une version en attente n'accorde rien (`NO_PERMISSIONS`), donc toutes ses permissions apparaissent comme nouvelles à l'écran 30.

Run: `bun test packages/daemon/src/ai/methods.test.ts packages/daemon/src/ai/adapters.test.ts` — Expected: PASS.

- [x] **Step 5: Composer l'IA (`bootstrap.ts`)**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/bootstrap.ts` :
```ts
import type { Database } from "bun:sqlite";
import { existsSync, mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, realpathSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
import { delimiter, join } from "node:path";
import {
  hashSources,
  inferPermissions,
  scaffold,
  type Toolchain,
  toolchainModules,
  validateComponent,
} from "@kibo/devkit";
import {
  BUILTIN_IDS,
  type ComponentSummary,
  Instance,
  type IntegrationStatus,
  KiboError,
  type ValidationReport,
} from "@kibo/schema";
import { listPages } from "@kibo/core";
import type { Orchestrator } from "../agents/orchestrator-types";
import { resolveClaudeBin } from "../agents/runner";
import { DIFF_FLAGS } from "../code/read";
import { editorCommand, openInEditor } from "../code/editor";
import { parseDiff } from "../code/parse-diff";
import { run } from "../code/run";
import { draftsDir } from "../components/drafts";
import type { Publisher } from "../components/publish";
import type { RegistryService } from "../components/registry-service";
import { type Docs, USER_COMMAND } from "../docs";
import { ensureSystemProfiles, readConfig } from "../workspace-config";
import {
  catalogEntries,
  createExecPort,
  githubConnected,
  kiboShimArgv,
  latestPublished,
  runsFromOrchestrator,
  systemClock,
  usagesOf,
  writeKiboShim,
} from "./adapters";
import { assistantArgs, type ClaudeProbe, createAiAvailability, generatorArgs, parseHelp, probeClaude } from "./claude-cli";
import { copySource, readDraftManifest, writeDraftManifest } from "./draft-files";
import { createDraftLifecycle } from "./draft-lifecycle";
import { grantedFromKeys } from "./draft-permissions";
import { createDraftPublisher } from "./draft-publish";
import { openDraftStore } from "./draft-store";
import { readEnvironment } from "./environment";
import { type AiPort, createAiRpc } from "./methods";
import type { AiEvents, ComponentCatalog, Devkit, Differ, ProjectAccess } from "./ports";
import { assistantPrompt, STARTER_PLAN_JSON_SCHEMA } from "./prompts";
import { createStarterService } from "./starter";

export type AiBootstrapDeps = {
  home: string;
  toolchain: Toolchain;
  db: Database;
  docs: Docs;
  orchestrator: Orchestrator;
  registry: RegistryService;
  publisher: Publisher;
  validate: ((dir: string, signal: AbortSignal) => Promise<ValidationReport>) | null;
  claudeBin: string | null;
  agentEnv: Record<string, string | undefined>;
  address: string;
  listIntegrations: () => Promise<IntegrationStatus[]>;
  assistantTimeoutMs?: number;
};

const MISSING: ClaudeProbe = { found: false, version: null, loggedIn: null, capabilities: null };

function claudeBinOrNull(configured: string | null, env: Record<string, string | undefined>): string | null {
  try {
    return resolveClaudeBin(configured, env);
  } catch (e) {
    if (e instanceof KiboError && e.code === "AGENT_CLI_NOT_FOUND") return null;
    throw e;
  }
}

function devkitPort(deps: AiBootstrapDeps, signal: AbortSignal): Devkit {
  const { home, toolchain } = deps;
  return {
    async scaffold({ dir, id, title, kind, withServer }) {
      mkdirSync(join(home, "tmp"), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
      const root = mkdtempSync(join(home, "tmp", "scaffold-"));
      try {
        copySource(await scaffold({ root, id, kind, server: withServer, toolchain }), dir);
        writeDraftManifest(dir, { ...readDraftManifest(dir), title, description: title });
      } finally {
        rmSync(root, { recursive: true, force: true });
      }
    },
    async infer(dir) {
      const { used, issues } = await inferPermissions(dir, toolchain);
      if (issues.length > 0) throw new KiboError("VALIDATION_FAILED", `${issues.length} source issue(s)`);
      return grantedFromKeys(used);
    },
    validate: (dir) => (deps.validate ? deps.validate(dir, signal) : validateComponent(dir, { toolchain, signal })),
    hash: (dir) => hashSources(dir),
  };
}

function catalogPort(deps: AiBootstrapDeps): ComponentCatalog {
  const list = (): ComponentSummary[] => deps.registry.list();
  const sourceDir = (id: string) => join(draftsDir(deps.home), id);
  return {
    entries: () => catalogEntries(list()),
    isTaken: (id) => BUILTIN_IDS.includes(id) || list().some((c) => c.id === id) || existsSync(sourceDir(id)),
    sourceDir,
    latest: (id) => latestPublished(list(), id),
    usages: (id) => usagesOf(list(), id),
    publish: (input) => deps.publisher.publish(input.id, input.strategy, { origin: input.origin }),
    approve: (input) => deps.registry.approve(input.id, input.version, input.hash, input.trust),
  };
}

function projectsPort(docs: Docs): ProjectAccess {
  return {
    pageExists: (projectId, pageId) =>
      docs.projectIds().includes(projectId) && listPages(docs.project(projectId)).some((p) => p.id === pageId),
    addInstance: async (projectId, pageId, ref) =>
      Instance.parse(docs.run(projectId, { method: "addInstance", pageId, component: ref }, USER_COMMAND)),
  };
}

function differPort(env: Record<string, string | undefined>, cwd: string): Differ {
  return async ({ path, before, after }) => {
    const r = await run([env.KIBO_GIT ?? "git", "diff", "--no-index", ...DIFF_FLAGS, "--", before ?? "/dev/null", after ?? "/dev/null"], { cwd });
    if (r.code > 1) throw new KiboError("GIT_FAILED", `git diff: ${r.stderr.split("\n")[0] ?? ""}`);
    return { ...parseDiff(r.stdout, path, null), hunkStaging: false };
  };
}

export async function startAi(deps: AiBootstrapDeps): Promise<{ port: AiPort; stop(): Promise<void> }> {
  const { home, docs, orchestrator, agentEnv, toolchain } = deps;
  const shutdown = new AbortController();
  ensureSystemProfiles(docs);
  const exec = createExecPort(agentEnv);
  const bin = claudeBinOrNull(deps.claudeBin, agentEnv);
  const ai = createAiAvailability({
    probe: () => (bin ? probeClaude(exec, bin) : Promise.resolve(MISSING)),
    profileEnabled: (id) => readConfig(docs).profiles.find((p) => p.id === id)?.enabled ?? false,
  });
  await ai.refresh();
  const events: AiEvents = { publish: (e) => docs.emit(e) };
  const runs = runsFromOrchestrator(orchestrator);
  const devkit = devkitPort(deps, shutdown.signal);
  const catalog = catalogPort(deps);
  const store = openDraftStore(deps.db);
  const binDir = writeKiboShim(join(home, "bin"), kiboShimArgv());
  const caps = () => ai.capabilities() ?? parseHelp("");
  const lifecycle = createDraftLifecycle({
    store,
    runs,
    devkit,
    catalog,
    ai,
    events,
    clock: systemClock,
    editor: { openFolder: async (dir) => openInEditor(editorCommand(dir, null, process.env, process.platform)) },
    home,
    sdkDir: realpathSync(join(toolchainModules(toolchain), "@kibo", "sdk")),
    args: () => generatorArgs(caps()),
    env: () => ({ PATH: `${binDir}${delimiter}${agentEnv.PATH ?? ""}`, KIBO_TOOLCHAIN: toolchain.root }),
    newId: () => crypto.randomUUID(),
  });
  const publisher = createDraftPublisher({
    store,
    devkit,
    catalog,
    projects: projectsPort(docs),
    differ: differPort(agentEnv, home),
    events,
    clock: systemClock,
    home,
  });
  mkdirSync(join(home, "tmp"), { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
  const starter = createStarterService({
    runs,
    ai,
    events,
    clock: systemClock,
    workRoot: join(home, "tmp"),
    catalog: () => catalog.entries(),
    prompt: assistantPrompt,
    args: () => assistantArgs(caps(), JSON.stringify(STARTER_PLAN_JSON_SCHEMA)),
    ...(deps.assistantTimeoutMs !== undefined && { timeoutMs: deps.assistantTimeoutMs }),
  });
  const port = createAiRpc({
    ai,
    starter,
    lifecycle,
    publisher,
    environment: () =>
      readEnvironment({
        daemon: { address: deps.address, home },
        refreshAi: () => ai.refresh(),
        exec,
        gitBin: agentEnv.KIBO_GIT ?? "git",
        ghBin: agentEnv.KIBO_GH ?? "gh",
        capacity: () => {
          const h = orchestrator.state().host;
          return { cores: h.cores, ramGb: h.ramGb, hostSlots: h.hostSlots };
        },
        githubConnected: async () => githubConnected(await deps.listIntegrations()),
      }),
  });
  await lifecycle.recover();
  return {
    port,
    async stop() {
      shutdown.abort();
      await lifecycle.idle();
    },
  };
}
```

- [x] **Step 7: Harnais et test d'intégration de l'onboarding**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/testing/harness.ts` :
```ts
import { mkdirSync, mkdtempSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { DEV_TOOLCHAIN } from "@kibo/devkit/test-kit";
import type { ChangeMessage, ComponentDraftDetails, DraftStatus, RpcRequest, RpcResult } from "@kibo/schema";
import { FAKE_CLAUDE } from "../../agents/fake-claude-scenario";
import { aiScenarioPath } from "../../agents/fake-claude-ai";
import { startDaemon } from "../../daemon";

export type AiHarness = {
  home: string;
  fakeState: string;
  rpc<R extends RpcRequest>(req: R): Promise<RpcResult[R["method"]]>;
  events: ChangeMessage[];
  waitFor(pred: (e: ChangeMessage) => boolean, ms?: number): Promise<ChangeMessage>;
  waitDraft(draftId: string, status: DraftStatus, ms?: number): Promise<ComponentDraftDetails>;
  stop(): Promise<void>;
};

type Envelope = { ok: true; result: unknown } | { ok: false; error: { code: string; message: string } };

async function pair(url: string, token: string): Promise<string> {
  const res = await fetch(`${url}/api/pair`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", origin: url },
    body: JSON.stringify({ token }),
  });
  if (res.status !== 204) throw new Error(`pairing failed: ${res.status}`);
  return res.headers.get("set-cookie")?.split(";")[0] ?? "";
}

export async function startAiHarness(opts: {
  scenario: string;
  claudeBin?: string;
  assistantTimeoutMs?: number;
}): Promise<AiHarness> {
  const home = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), "kibo-ai-"));
  const fakeState = join(home, "fake");
  mkdirSync(fakeState);
  const daemon = await startDaemon({
    home,
    port: 0,
    sandboxPort: 0,
    uiDir: null,
    dev: false,
    toolchain: DEV_TOOLCHAIN,
    user: "adam",
    claudeBin: opts.claudeBin ?? FAKE_CLAUDE,
    sampler: () => ({ cpu: 5, ram: 5 }),
    agentEnv: {
      PATH: process.env.PATH,
      HOME: process.env.HOME,
      KIBO_FAKE_CLAUDE_SCENARIO: aiScenarioPath(opts.scenario),
      KIBO_FAKE_CLAUDE_STATE: fakeState,
    },
    assistantTimeoutMs: opts.assistantTimeoutMs ?? 1_000,
  });
  const cookie = await pair(daemon.url, daemon.token);
  const events: ChangeMessage[] = [];
  const waiters = new Set<() => void>();
  const ws = new WebSocket(`${daemon.url.replace("http", "ws")}/api/events`, { headers: { cookie, origin: daemon.url } });
  ws.onmessage = (m) => {
    events.push(JSON.parse(String(m.data)));
    for (const w of [...waiters]) w();
  };
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    ws.onopen = () => resolve();
    ws.onerror = () => reject(new Error("event socket failed"));
  });
  const post = async (req: RpcRequest): Promise<unknown> => {
    const res = await fetch(`${daemon.url}/api/rpc`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json", origin: daemon.url, cookie },
      body: JSON.stringify(req),
    });
    const body: Envelope = await res.json();
    if (!body.ok) throw new Error(`${body.error.code}: ${body.error.message}`);
    return body.result;
  };
  const rpc = <R extends RpcRequest>(req: R) => post(req) as Promise<RpcResult[R["method"]]>;
  const waitFor = (pred: (e: ChangeMessage) => boolean, ms = 10_000) =>
    new Promise<ChangeMessage>((resolve, reject) => {
      const check = () => {
        const hit = events.find(pred);
        if (!hit) return;
        waiters.delete(check);
        clearTimeout(timer);
        resolve(hit);
      };
      const timer = setTimeout(() => {
        waiters.delete(check);
        reject(new Error("timeout waiting for a daemon event"));
      }, ms);
      waiters.add(check);
      check();
    });
  return {
    home,
    fakeState,
    rpc,
    events,
    waitFor,
    async waitDraft(draftId, status, ms = 120_000) {
      const deadline = Date.now() + ms;
      for (;;) {
        const d = await rpc({ method: "getComponentDraft", draftId });
        if (d.status === status) return d;
        if (Date.now() > deadline) throw new Error(`draft ${draftId} is ${d.status}, expected ${status}`);
        await Bun.sleep(100);
      }
    },
    async stop() {
      ws.close();
      await daemon.stop();
      rmSync(home, { recursive: true, force: true });
    },
  };
}
```
(Le `as` de `rpc` et le `JSON.parse` non validé sont du code de test : l'enveloppe vient du démon testé.) `server.ts` diffuse chaque `ChangeMessage` tel quel (`JSON.stringify(message)`) sur `/api/events`.

`packages/daemon/src/ai/onboarding.int.test.ts` (scénario 6 de la spec) :
```ts
import { afterEach, expect, test } from "bun:test";
import { fakeCalls } from "../agents/fake-claude-scenario";
import { type AiHarness, startAiHarness } from "./testing/harness";

let h: AiHarness | null = null;
afterEach(async () => {
  await h?.stop();
  h = null;
});

const ready = (runId: string) => (e: { type?: string; runId?: string }) => e.type === "starter.ready" && e.runId === runId;

test("valid JSON ⇒ plan, and the assistant runs without tools nor skipped permissions", async () => {
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "onboarding-ok.json" });
  expect(await h.rpc({ method: "getAiStatus" })).toMatchObject({ available: true, profiles: { assistant: true, generateur: true } });
  const { runId } = await h.rpc({ method: "suggestStarter", role: "other", text: "Je suis freelance" });
  expect(await h.waitFor(ready(runId))).toMatchObject({ plan: { pages: [{ title: "Suivi clients" }, { title: "Tickets" }] } });
  const agents = await h.rpc({ method: "getAgents" });
  const sessionId = agents.runs.find((r) => r.id === runId)?.sessionId ?? "";
  const argv = fakeCalls(h.fakeState, sessionId)[0]?.argv ?? [];
  expect(argv).toContain("--tools");
  expect(argv[argv.indexOf("--tools") + 1]).toBe("");
  expect(argv).toContain("--json-schema");
  expect(argv.join(" ")).not.toContain("dangerously");
  expect(argv.join(" ")).not.toContain("bypassPermissions");
});

for (const [scenario, why] of [
  ["onboarding-invalid.json", "prose"],
  ["onboarding-unknown.json", "unknown component"],
  ["onboarding-slow.json", "timeout"],
] as const)
  test(`${why} ⇒ null plan (preset)`, async () => {
    h = await startAiHarness({ scenario });
    const { runId } = await h.rpc({ method: "suggestStarter", role: "other", text: "x" });
    expect(await h.waitFor(ready(runId), 15_000)).toMatchObject({ plan: null });
  });

test("claude missing ⇒ AI_UNAVAILABLE", async () => {
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "onboarding-ok.json", claudeBin: "/nonexistent/claude" });
  expect(await h.rpc({ method: "getAiStatus" })).toMatchObject({ available: false, reason: "missing" });
  await expect(h.rpc({ method: "suggestStarter", role: "other", text: "x" })).rejects.toThrow("AI_UNAVAILABLE");
});

test("the system profiles exist and cannot be deleted", async () => {
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "onboarding-ok.json" });
  const config = await h.rpc({ method: "getConfig" });
  expect(config.profiles.filter((p) => p.system).map((p) => p.id).sort()).toEqual(["assistant", "generateur"]);
});
```
Un `claudeBin` qui n'existe pas : `resolveClaudeBin` rend le chemin configuré tel quel ; le sondage (`createExecPort`) le trouve absent et rend `null` ⇒ `missing` (vérifier ce chemin ; si `probeClaude` lève sur `null`, corriger T2 plutôt que ce test). Le délai de l'assistant vaut 1 s dans le harnais (`onboarding-slow` dort 5 s).

### Task 18: Scénarios de génération avec le faux `claude` (spec §8, 1 à 5 et modification)

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/daemon/src/ai/generation.int.test.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/agents/scenarios/ai/modify-routes.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `startAiHarness` (T17) ; scénarios et fixtures `agents/scenarios/ai/` (T9) ; `fakeCalls` (`agents/fake-claude-scenario.ts`, phase 2) et `fakeWrites` (T9) ; RPC `createProject`, `command` (`addPage`), `getProject`, `listComponents`.
- Produces: les six scénarios de sortie, exécutés localement sans réseau ni token (CI GitHub hors service : contrôle local du chef d'équipe).

Contexte réel : le journal du faux `claude` est par session (`<STATE>/<sessionId>.calls.jsonl` : `argv`, `cwd`, `prompt` lu sur l'entrée standard ; `<sessionId>.writes.jsonl` : écritures et refus, T9). La session d'un brouillon est `ComponentDraftDetails.sessionId`, fixée par Kibo au premier run et reprise par `--resume` (I28). Les routes du faux `claude` se choisissent par sous-chaîne du prompt (`{ prompt, scenario }`), et une session reprise garde son scénario. Le mode de permission est posé par le runner (`--permission-mode acceptEdits` pour `generateur`).

- [x] **Step 1: Scénario de modification routé**

`packages/daemon/src/agents/scenarios/ai/modify-routes.json` :
```json
{ "routes": [
  { "prompt": "Modifie le composant Kibo", "scenario": "modify-ok.json" },
  { "prompt": "Écris le composant Kibo", "scenario": "generate-ok.json" } ],
  "fallback": "generate-ok.json" }
```
Les deux sous-chaînes sont celles des prompts de T5 (`generatorPrompt` en mode `modify` et `create`) : les vérifier dans `prompts.ts` et les recopier à l'identique.

- [x] **Step 2: Écrire les scénarios**

`packages/daemon/src/ai/generation.int.test.ts` :
```ts
import { afterEach, expect, setDefaultTimeout, test } from "bun:test";
import { existsSync, readFileSync, realpathSync } from "node:fs";
import { join, relative } from "node:path";
import { fakeWrites } from "../agents/fake-claude-ai";
import { fakeCalls } from "../agents/fake-claude-scenario";
import { type AiHarness, startAiHarness } from "./testing/harness";

setDefaultTimeout(180_000);
let h: AiHarness | null = null;
afterEach(async () => {
  await h?.stop();
  h = null;
});

async function projectWithPage(harness: AiHarness) {
  const p = await harness.rpc({ method: "createProject", name: "Kibo", key: "KIB", folder: null, color: "#14B8A6" });
  await harness.rpc({ method: "command", projectId: p.id, command: { method: "addPage", title: "Tableau de bord", kind: "dashboard" } });
  const project = await harness.rpc({ method: "getProject", projectId: p.id });
  const page = project.pages.find((x) => x.title === "Tableau de bord");
  if (!page) throw new Error("page not created");
  return { projectId: p.id, pageId: page.id };
}

const create = {
  mode: "create",
  id: "burndown",
  title: "Burndown",
  kind: "widget",
  withServer: false,
  description: "Burndown du sprint : tickets restants par jour.",
} as const;

async function toReview(harness: AiHarness) {
  const draft = await harness.rpc({ method: "startComponentDraft", draft: create });
  return harness.waitDraft(draft.id, "review");
}

async function finalize(
  harness: AiHarness,
  draftId: string,
  target: { projectId: string; pageId: string } | null,
  version = "0.1.0",
  changes: string[] = [],
) {
  const reviewed = await harness.rpc({ method: "reviewComponentDraft", draftId, version, changes });
  const hash = reviewed.publish?.hash ?? "";
  return harness.rpc({ method: "finalizeComponentDraft", draftId, version, hash, trust: "sandboxed", strategy: "update-all", target });
}

const callsOf = (harness: AiHarness, sessionId: string | null) => fakeCalls(harness.fakeState, sessionId ?? "");

test("1 · first-time success ⇒ review ⇒ finalize ⇒ sandboxed instance on the page", async () => {
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "generate-ok.json" });
  const target = await projectWithPage(h);
  const review = await toReview(h);
  expect(review.diff.map((f) => f.path)).toEqual(["ui.tsx"]);
  expect(review.report?.ok).toBe(true);
  expect(review.manifest?.reads).toContain("ticket");
  expect(review.publish).toMatchObject({ status: "new", to: "0.1.0", from: null });
  const result = await finalize(h, review.id, target);
  expect(result).toMatchObject({ version: { version: "0.1.0", origin: "ai", trust: "sandboxed" } });
  expect(result.instanceId).not.toBeNull();
  const project = await h.rpc({ method: "getProject", projectId: target.projectId });
  expect(project.instances.map((i) => i.component)).toContain("burndown@0.1.0");
  const burndown = (await h.rpc({ method: "listComponents" })).find((c) => c.id === "burndown");
  expect(burndown?.versions[0]).toMatchObject({ origin: "ai", trust: "sandboxed", active: true });
  expect(existsSync(join(h.home, "components", "drafts", review.id))).toBe(false);
  expect(existsSync(join(h.home, "components", "src", "burndown", "CLAUDE.md"))).toBe(false);
  const argv = callsOf(h, review.sessionId)[0]?.argv ?? [];
  expect(argv[argv.indexOf("--tools") + 1]).toBe("Read,Edit,Write,Glob,Grep,Bash");
  expect(argv[argv.indexOf("--permission-mode") + 1]).toBe("acceptEdits");
  expect(argv.join(" ")).not.toContain("dangerously");
});

test("2 · typecheck failure, then success after --resume with the report", async () => {
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "generate-retry.json" });
  const draft = await h.rpc({ method: "startComponentDraft", draft: create });
  const failed = await h.waitDraft(draft.id, "failed");
  expect(failed.report?.typecheck.ok).toBe(false);
  await h.rpc({ method: "retryComponentDraft", draftId: draft.id });
  const review = await h.waitDraft(draft.id, "review");
  expect(review.attempts).toBe(2);
  const [first, second] = callsOf(h, review.sessionId);
  expect(first?.argv).toContain("--session-id");
  expect(second?.argv).toContain("--resume");
  expect(second?.prompt).toContain("error TS");
});

test("3 · three failures ⇒ retry refused", async () => {
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "generate-fail-3.json" });
  const draft = await h.rpc({ method: "startComponentDraft", draft: create });
  for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
    await h.waitDraft(draft.id, "failed");
    await h.rpc({ method: "retryComponentDraft", draftId: draft.id });
  }
  const last = await h.waitDraft(draft.id, "failed");
  expect(last.attempts).toBe(3);
  await expect(h.rpc({ method: "retryComponentDraft", draftId: draft.id })).rejects.toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
});

test("4 · guard denies reserved and outside writes; direct tampering is restored", async () => {
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "generate-guard.json" });
  const review = await toReview(h);
  const dir = realpathSync(join(h.home, "components", "drafts", review.id));
  const writes = fakeWrites(h.fakeState, review.sessionId ?? "");
  const denied = writes.flatMap((w) => ("denied" in w ? [relative(dir, w.denied)] : []));
  expect(denied).toEqual(["kibo.component.json", join("..", "evil.ts")]);
  expect(existsSync(join(h.home, "components", "drafts", "evil.ts"))).toBe(false);
  expect(review.incidents).toEqual([
    { kind: "removed", path: "evil.ts" },
    { kind: "restored", path: "kibo.component.json" },
  ]);
  const manifest = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(h.home, "components", "drafts", review.id, "kibo.component.json"), "utf8"));
  expect(manifest).toMatchObject({ id: "burndown", version: "0.1.0", writes: [] });
  expect(manifest.net ?? []).toEqual([]);
});

test("5 · literal sdk.fetch ⇒ declared net permission; non-literal ⇒ validation failure", async () => {
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "generate-fetch.json" });
  const review = await toReview(h);
  expect(review.manifest?.net).toEqual(["api.github.com/repos/kibo/kibo/issues"]);
  expect(review.publish?.newPermissions).toContain("net:api.github.com/repos/kibo/kibo/issues");
  await h.stop();
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "generate-fetch-dynamic.json" });
  const draft = await h.rpc({ method: "startComponentDraft", draft: create });
  const failed = await h.waitDraft(draft.id, "failed");
  expect(failed.report?.permissions.errors.join("\n")).toContain("non-literal");
});

test("modify · minor version proposed, published everywhere", async () => {
  h = await startAiHarness({ scenario: "modify-routes.json" });
  const target = await projectWithPage(h);
  const created = await toReview(h);
  await finalize(h, created.id, target);
  const draft = await h.rpc({
    method: "startComponentDraft",
    draft: { mode: "modify", id: "burndown", description: "Affiche le nombre d'issues" },
  });
  const review = await h.waitDraft(draft.id, "review");
  expect(review.publish).toMatchObject({ from: "0.1.0", to: "0.2.0", status: "update", changes: ["Affiche le nombre d'issues"] });
  expect(review.publish?.usages.map((u) => u.version)).toEqual(["0.1.0"]);
  await finalize(h, draft.id, null, "0.2.0", ["Affiche le nombre d'issues"]);
  const project = await h.rpc({ method: "getProject", projectId: target.projectId });
  expect(project.instances.map((i) => i.component)).toContain("burndown@0.2.0");
});
```
Le texte de l'erreur d'une inférence non littérale est celui de `formatIssue` du devkit (`non-literal-argument`) : ajuster la sous-chaîne à la sortie réelle, sans affaiblir l'assertion. Le chemin exact d'une URL dans les fixtures `ui-fetch*.tsx` de T9 fixe la règle `net` attendue (I25 : `hôte/chemin`).

### Task 19: Parcours Playwright IA (sombre et clair)

**Files:**
- Create: `e2e/ia.spec.ts`
- Modify: `e2e/playwright.config.ts` (projets `ia-dark` et `ia-light`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: faux `claude` et `agents/scenarios/ai/e2e-routes.json` (T9) ; écrans des tâches 11, 12, 13 ; `skipRoleStep` (`e2e/helpers.ts`, T13) ; `TrustDialog` (textes `fr.components.trust`) ; iframe sandboxée (`shell/SandboxFrame.tsx`, `sandbox="allow-scripts"`).
- Produces: parcours « Ton rôle » (preset et suggestion) et « Créer un composant avec un agent » jusqu'au rendu sandboxé, dans les projets Playwright `ia-dark` et `ia-light`.

Contexte réel (E5) : `e2e/serve.ts <port> <scénario>` lance déjà `main.ts --claude-bin packages/daemon/src/agents/fake-claude.ts` avec `KIBO_FAKE_CLAUDE_SCENARIO = agents/scenarios/<scénario>.json` et `KIBO_FAKE_CLAUDE_STATE` ; le scénario `ai/e2e-routes` désigne donc `agents/scenarios/ai/e2e-routes.json` **sans modifier `serve.ts`**. Le démon hérite de cet environnement (I37 : `agentEnv` vaut `process.env`), le faux `claude` répond à `--version`, `--help` et `auth status` (T9) : l'IA est disponible. Chaque projet Playwright a son démon ; les ports 4390 à 4403 sont pris (4402 et 4403 par les intégrations), le faux GitHub prend `port + 1000` et le faux MCP `port + 2000`.

- [x] **Step 1: Projets Playwright**

Dans `e2e/playwright.config.ts`, ajouter à `daemons` (ports suivants libres au moment de la tâche, 4404 et 4405 à ce jour ; vérifier que la phase 5 n'en a pas pris d'autres) :
```ts
  { name: "ia-dark", scheme: "dark", port: 4404, spec: /ia\.spec\.ts/, scenario: "ai/e2e-routes" },
  { name: "ia-light", scheme: "light", port: 4405, spec: /ia\.spec\.ts/, scenario: "ai/e2e-routes" },
```

- [x] **Step 2: Écrire le parcours**

`e2e/ia.spec.ts` :
```ts
import { expect, type Page, type TestInfo, test } from "@playwright/test";
import { skipRoleStep } from "./helpers";
import { E2E_TOKEN } from "./token";

const suffix = (info: TestInfo) => (info.project.name.endsWith("light") ? "L" : "D");

async function pair(page: Page, info: TestInfo) {
  await page.goto(`/#pair=${E2E_TOKEN}`);
  const html = page.locator("html");
  if (info.project.name.endsWith("dark")) await expect(html).toHaveClass(/dark/);
  else await expect(html).not.toHaveClass(/dark/);
}

async function openNewProject(page: Page) {
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: /Nouveau projet|Créer un projet/ }).first().click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toBeVisible();
}

async function finishProject(page: Page, name: string, key: string) {
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Continuer" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Pages conseillées" })).toHaveAttribute("data-state", "checked");
  await page.getByLabel("Nom").fill(name);
  await page.getByLabel("Clé").fill(key);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Créer le projet" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByRole("dialog")).toBeHidden();
}

test("Ton rôle : preset Designer, une page décochée", async ({ page }, info) => {
  const s = suffix(info);
  await pair(page, info);
  await openNewProject(page);
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Designer" }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Inclure Kanban").click();
  await finishProject(page, `Design ${s}`, `DSG${s}`);
  const project = page.getByRole("navigation").getByRole("listitem").filter({ hasText: `Design ${s}` }).first();
  await expect(project.getByText("Tableau de bord")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(project.getByText("Notes")).toBeVisible();
  await expect(project.getByText("Kanban", { exact: true })).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(page.getByText(/Mes tickets · 0 sur 0/)).toBeVisible();
});

test("Ton rôle : suggestion de Claude (faux claude)", async ({ page }, info) => {
  const s = suffix(info);
  await pair(page, info);
  await openNewProject(page);
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Autre" }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Décris ton usage en une phrase").fill("Je suis freelance et je suis trois clients");
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Proposer avec Claude" }).click();
  await expect(page.getByText("Proposition de Claude")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 30_000 });
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Nom de la page 1")).toHaveValue("Suivi clients");
  await finishProject(page, `Freelance ${s}`, `FRL${s}`);
  await expect(page.getByRole("navigation").getByText("Suivi clients")).toBeVisible();
});

test("Créer un composant avec un agent, jusqu'au rendu sandboxé", async ({ page }, info) => {
  test.setTimeout(180_000);
  const s = suffix(info);
  await pair(page, info);
  await openNewProject(page);
  await skipRoleStep(page);
  await page.getByLabel("Nom").fill(`Composants ${s}`);
  await page.getByLabel("Clé").fill(`CMP${s}`);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Créer le projet" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("main").getByRole("button", { name: "Nouvelle page" }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Nom").fill("Tableau de bord");
  await page.getByRole("radio", { name: "Tableau de bord", exact: true }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Créer la page" }).click();

  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Ajouter un composant" }).click();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Créer un composant" }).click();
  await page.getByLabel("Ce que doit faire le composant").fill("Burndown du sprint : tickets restants par jour, ligne idéale.");
  await page.getByLabel("Titre").fill(`Burndown ${s}`);
  await expect(page.getByLabel("Identifiant")).toHaveValue(`burndown-${s.toLowerCase()}`);
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Générer avec un agent" }).click();

  await expect(page.getByText("Relire le diff")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 120_000 });
  await expect(page.getByRole("button", { name: "ui.tsx" })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole("button", { name: "J'ai relu, continuer" }).click();

  const trust = page.getByRole("dialog", { name: new RegExp(`Autoriser « Burndown ${s} » 0\\.1\\.0 \\?`) });
  await expect(trust.getByText(/Composant généré par IA/)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(trust.getByRole("radio", { name: /Sandboxé \(recommandé\)/ })).toBeChecked();
  await trust.getByRole("button", { name: "Autoriser et ajouter" }).click();
  await expect(trust).toBeHidden();

  const frame = page.frameLocator("iframe[sandbox='allow-scripts']");
  await expect(frame.getByText("tickets restants")).toBeVisible({ timeout: 30_000 });
});
```

Les libellés (« Inclure Kanban », « Nom de la page 1 », « Ce que doit faire le composant », « Générer avec un agent », « Relire le diff », « J'ai relu, continuer ») sont ceux de `fr-ai.ts` (T1) et des composants de T12 et T13 : les aligner au mot près sur le code livré. Le composant généré par le faux `claude` est la fixture `burndown/ui.tsx` de T9 (texte « tickets restants »).

- Create: `packages/ui/src/agents/SystemProfileFields.tsx`

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `ProfileInput.enabled: boolean` (défaut `true`) ; `AgentProfile.system: boolean` (défaut `false`) ; `SYSTEM_PROFILE_IDS = ["assistant", "generateur"] as const` et `type SystemProfileId` (`@kibo/schema`) ; `systemModel(profiles: AgentProfile[]): AgentModel` et `ensureSystemProfiles(ws: LoroDoc): boolean` (`@kibo/core/agent-config`) ; `ensureSystemProfiles(docs: Docs): void` (`daemon/src/workspace-config.ts`, enregistre et émet `{ topic: "config" }` seulement si quelque chose a changé) ; `SystemProfileFields({ profile, onSaved })`.

Décisions appliquées : I18 (ids fixes, `system`, `enabled`, modèle et activation seuls modifiables, ni suppression ni assignation), P1 (modèle initial = modèle du premier profil utilisateur par ordre alphabétique, sinon `sonnet` ; option appliquée en attendant Adam), P2 (UI sans maquette : badge « Système », seuls « Modèle » et « Activé »). Les valeurs fixes suivent la spec §4.1 : `assistant` = `default`, `isolated`, parallèle 1 ; `generateur` = `acceptEdits`, `isolated`, parallèle 1 ; `execution: "cli"`, `subagents: []`. Les profils déjà stockés (sans `system` ni `enabled`) se relisent avec les valeurs par défaut de Zod : aucune migration du CRDT.

- [x] **Step 1: Tests du domaine**

Ajouter à `packages/core/src/agent-config.test.ts` (même préparation du document que les tests existants, `ws` = document de workspace) :
```ts
describe("system profiles", () => {
  test("ensureSystemProfiles creates both profiles once, with the first user model", () => {
    executeConfigCommand(ws, { method: "createProfile", profile: { ...userProfile, name: "zed", model: "haiku" } });
    executeConfigCommand(ws, { method: "createProfile", profile: { ...userProfile, name: "alpha", model: "opus" } });
    expect(ensureSystemProfiles(ws)).toBe(true);
    expect(ensureSystemProfiles(ws)).toBe(false);
    const system = listProfiles(ws).filter((p) => p.system);
    expect(system.map((p) => [p.id, p.permissionMode, p.model, p.enabled, p.maxParallel])).toEqual([
      ["assistant", "default", "opus", true, 1],
      ["generateur", "acceptEdits", "opus", true, 1],
    ]);
  });

  test("without user profiles the model is sonnet; model and enabled survive a restart", () => {
    ensureSystemProfiles(ws);
    expect(getProfile(ws, "assistant").model).toBe("sonnet");
    executeConfigCommand(ws, { method: "updateProfile", profileId: "generateur", patch: { model: "opus", enabled: false } });
    ensureSystemProfiles(ws);
    expect(getProfile(ws, "generateur")).toMatchObject({ model: "opus", enabled: false, permissionMode: "acceptEdits" });
  });

  test("a system profile only changes its model and enabled flag, and cannot be deleted", () => {
    ensureSystemProfiles(ws);
    expect(() =>
      executeConfigCommand(ws, { method: "updateProfile", profileId: "assistant", patch: { permissionMode: "acceptEdits" } }),
    ).toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
    expect(() => executeConfigCommand(ws, { method: "updateProfile", profileId: "assistant", patch: { name: "x" } })).toThrow(
      "INVALID_INPUT",
    );
    expect(() => executeConfigCommand(ws, { method: "deleteProfile", profileId: "generateur" })).toThrow("INVALID_INPUT");
  });

  test("a stored profile written before phase 6 reads as a user profile, enabled", () => {
    ws.getMap("profiles").set("old", { ...userProfile, id: "old" });
    ws.commit();
    expect(getProfile(ws, "old")).toMatchObject({ system: false, enabled: true });
  });
});
```
(`userProfile` : le `ProfileInput` déjà utilisé par les tests du fichier ; s'il n'existe pas, le définir en tête : `{ name: "dev", model: "sonnet", execution: "cli", permissionMode: "acceptEdits", workspace: "worktree", maxParallel: 2, subagents: [], enabled: true }`.)

Run: `bun test packages/core/src/agent-config.test.ts` — Expected: FAIL (`ensureSystemProfiles` introuvable).

- [x] **Step 2: Schéma et domaine**

`packages/schema/src/agent.ts` :
```ts
export const ProfileInput = z.object({
  name: ProfileName,
  model: AgentModel,
  execution: z.literal("cli"),
  permissionMode: PermissionMode,
  workspace: WorkspaceStrategy,
  maxParallel: z.number().int().min(1).max(16),
  subagents: z.array(AgentModel),
  enabled: z.boolean().default(true),
});
export type ProfileInput = z.infer<typeof ProfileInput>;

export const AgentProfile = ProfileInput.extend({ id: z.string().min(1), system: z.boolean().default(false) });
export type AgentProfile = z.infer<typeof AgentProfile>;

export const SYSTEM_PROFILE_IDS = ["assistant", "generateur"] as const;
export type SystemProfileId = (typeof SYSTEM_PROFILE_IDS)[number];
```
`ConfigCommand.createProfile` garde `profile: ProfileInput` : un utilisateur ne peut pas créer de profil `system` (le champ n'existe pas dans `ProfileInput`, `AgentProfile.parse` le met à `false`).

`packages/core/src/agent-config.ts` :
```ts
const SYSTEM_FIELDS: Record<SystemProfileId, Pick<AgentProfile, "name" | "permissionMode">> = {
  assistant: { name: "assistant", permissionMode: "default" },
  generateur: { name: "generateur", permissionMode: "acceptEdits" },
};
const SYSTEM_EDITABLE = new Set(["model", "enabled"]);

export function systemModel(profiles: AgentProfile[]): AgentModel {
  return profiles.filter((p) => !p.system).sort(byName)[0]?.model ?? "sonnet";
}

export function ensureSystemProfiles(ws: LoroDoc): boolean {
  requireWorkspace(ws);
  const profiles = listProfiles(ws);
  let changed = false;
  for (const id of SYSTEM_PROFILE_IDS) {
    const current = profiles.find((p) => p.id === id) ?? null;
    const next = valid(
      AgentProfile.safeParse({
        ...SYSTEM_FIELDS[id],
        id,
        system: true,
        execution: "cli",
        workspace: "isolated",
        maxParallel: 1,
        subagents: [],
        model: current?.model ?? systemModel(profiles),
        enabled: current?.enabled ?? true,
      }),
    );
    if (current && JSON.stringify(current) === JSON.stringify(next)) continue;
    profilesMap(ws).set(id, next);
    changed = true;
  }
  if (changed) ws.commit();
  return changed;
}
```
Dans `executeConfigCommand` : `updateProfile` lève `KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", "only the model and the enabled flag of a system profile can change")` si `current.system` et qu'une clé de `cmd.patch` n'est pas dans `SYSTEM_EDITABLE` ; `deleteProfile` lève `KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", "a system profile cannot be deleted")` si le profil est `system`. `assertUniqueName` s'applique aux profils utilisateur contre tous les profils : un profil utilisateur ne peut plus s'appeler `assistant` ni `generateur` ; un profil existant qui portait déjà ce nom garde son nom tant qu'il n'est pas modifié.

Run: `bun test packages/core packages/schema` — Expected: PASS.

- [x] **Step 3: Démon (démarrage et refus d'assignation)**

`packages/daemon/src/workspace-config.ts` :
```ts
export function ensureSystemProfiles(docs: Docs): void {
  if (!ensureSystemProfilesIn(docs.workspace)) return;
  docs.save(null);
  docs.emit({ topic: "config" });
}
```
(`ensureSystemProfilesIn` = import renommé de `ensureSystemProfiles` depuis `@kibo/core/agent-config`.) L'appel au démarrage est fait par T17 (`startAi`) ; T20 le teste dans `service.test.ts` : deux appels n'émettent qu'une fois, `getConfig` rend les deux profils.

`packages/daemon/src/agents/orchestrator.ts` : `assign` et `preview` passent par `const ticketProfileOf = (id: string) => { const p = profileOf(id); if (p.system) throw new KiboError("INVALID_INPUT", `profile ${p.name} is reserved to Kibo`); return p; };` ; `submit` garde `profileOf` (les runs sans ticket de l'IA utilisent les profils système). Test ajouté à `orchestrator.test.ts` : `assign` et `preview` avec `profileId: "assistant"` lèvent `INVALID_INPUT` sans créer de run ; `submit` avec `profileId: "assistant"` crée un run sans ticket.

Run: `bun test packages/daemon/src/agents packages/daemon/src/service.test.ts` — Expected: PASS.

### Task 21: Origine de publication `ai` (phase 4)

**Tâche à risque** : relue par `kibo-lead` avant intégration (une version d'origine `ai` ne doit jamais hériter de la confiance, I22).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/daemon/src/components/publish.ts`, `packages/daemon/src/components/publish.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createPublisher`, `Publisher` (C3) ; `ComponentOrigin` (`@kibo/schema`).
- Produces: `Publisher.publish(id: string, strategy: "update-all" | "new-version", opts?: { origin: "user" | "ai" }): Promise<PublishResult>` (défaut `{ origin: "user" }`, comportement actuel inchangé).

- [x] **Step 1: Tests**

Ajouter à `packages/daemon/src/components/publish.test.ts`, avec la préparation des tests existants de l'héritage de confiance (une version `0.1.0` approuvée `sandboxed`, puis un brouillon `0.1.1` sans nouvelle permission) :
```ts
test("an ai version never inherits trust, even without new permissions", async () => {
  const result = await publisher.publish("hello", "update-all", { origin: "ai" });
  expect(result.needsApproval).toBe(true);
  expect(result.version).toMatchObject({ version: "0.1.1", origin: "ai", trust: null, approvedHash: null });
  expect(result.updated).toEqual([]);
});

test("a user version keeps inheriting trust (default origin)", async () => {
  const result = await publisher.publish("hello", "update-all");
  expect(result.version).toMatchObject({ origin: "user", trust: "sandboxed" });
  expect(result.needsApproval).toBe(false);
});
```
(Nommer `publisher` et `"hello"` comme dans les tests existants du fichier.)

Run: `bun test packages/daemon/src/components/publish.test.ts` — Expected: FAIL (troisième argument inconnu, origine `user`).

- [x] **Step 2: Implémenter**

Dans `publish.ts` :
- `Publisher.publish(id, strategy, opts?: { origin: "user" | "ai" })` ;
- `const origin = opts?.origin ?? "user";` ;
- `inheritedTrust` renvoie `null` si `origin === "ai"` (avant les autres conditions) ;
- `version.origin = origin` au lieu de `"user"`.
Le chemin `unchanged` (même version, même empreinte) rend la version existante sans changer son origine.

## Jalon v0.6

Pas d'attente de validation : Adam a décidé d'enchaîner jusqu'à la phase 7 ; le rapport est écrit, puis la phase 7 démarre.

- [ ] `main` verte au contrôle local (CI GitHub hors service) : `bun run check`, `bun run typecheck`, `bun test packages components` (dont `onboarding.int.test.ts` et `generation.int.test.ts`), build UI, `bun run budget` (entrée sous 230 kB gzip, valeur relevée dans le rapport), Playwright sombre et clair (tous les projets), smoke Tauri.
- [ ] **Critères de sortie de la spec §9** : scénarios 1 à 6 verts sans réseau ni token ; composant généré rendu sandboxé sur une page et conforme ; écrans conformes.
- [ ] **Conformité visuelle**, en sombre et en clair, face à `design/pdf/` et aux écrans ajoutés dans Penpot : 19 (Bienvenue), 29 (les deux colonnes, étapes 1 à 5, états d'échec et d'épuisement, relecture du diff), 30 (origine IA, Sandboxé présélectionné), 6 (mode brouillon IA), Nouveau projet étapes 1 et 2, Suggérer des pages (25, 26), Modifier avec l'IA. Écarts listés dans le rapport.
- [ ] Essai manuel facultatif avec un vrai `claude` connecté (consomme des tokens d'Adam) : **non fait par l'équipe**, noté dans le rapport comme test restant.
- [ ] Penpot réexporté (`kibo.penpot.xz`, `design/pdf/`) avec les écrans ajoutés (dont les profils système, P2) ; texte de l'écran 29 corrigé (I14).
- [ ] Points P1 à P4 rappelés dans le rapport avec l'option appliquée, pour décision d'Adam.
- [ ] Feuille de route : lien vers ce plan dans la ligne de la phase 6.
- [ ] Tag `v0.6`, puis rapport `docs/superpowers/rapports/2026-09-26-jalon-v0.6.md` (livré, écarts, risques ci-dessous, suite = phase 7), commit `docs: rapport du jalon v0.6`.

### Risques à reporter dans le rapport

- **Contrats amont** : T0 a aligné le plan sur `main` (phase 5 jusqu'à sa tâche 22) ; les runs sans ticket, le garde-fou et la sortie capturée existent (A1, A2). Deux modifications de phases livrées restent : T20 (profils système) et T21 (origine `ai`).
- **Reprise d'une finalisation partielle** : si l'ajout de l'instance échoue après la publication, une seconde finalisation peut ajouter une deuxième instance (T15).
- **Run de l'assistant perdu au redémarrage** : sans effet côté démon ; l'UI conclut « indisponible » à la reconnexion et retombe sur le preset (I41).
- **Sortie JSON de `claude`** : la forme exacte de `structured_output` n'a pas été vérifiée sans consommer de tokens ; le parseur accepte aussi `result` texte, bloc ```` ```json ```` et JSON nu.
- **Mode `default`** : la CLI 2.1.283 ne liste plus `default` mais `manual` ; le repli est testé, mais un renommage futur de `acceptEdits` ferait tomber le générateur en `AI_UNAVAILABLE`-like (run en échec).
- **Garde-fou si le démon meurt en plein run** : les hooks d'un `claude` orphelin échouent ; la phase 2 doit tuer le groupe de processus ; au redémarrage, `recover` restaure les fichiers réservés.
- **Confinement des lectures** : `Read`/`Glob`/`Grep` sont confinés par le hook ; un `Bash` autorisé (`kibo component test .`) exécute le code généré sous les restrictions de la phase 4 (sans bac à sable OS avant la phase 7).
- **Durée de la validation** (tsc + tests) dans les scénarios et l'E2E : délais généreux (120 à 180 s).
- **Budget du chargement initial** : environ 215 kB sur 230 kB avant la phase ; tout écran nouveau est différé (I29), mais la marge reste faible pour la phase 7.
